}}\n\n==Background==\nA light heavyweight rematch between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Magomed Ankalaev]] and [[Johnny Walker (fighter)|Johnny Walker]] headlined the event. The pairing originally met at [[UFC 294]] which ended in a no contest when Ankalaev landed an illegal knee that rendered Walker unable to continue.{{cite web |author=Dewar |first=Val |date=October 21, 2023 |title=UFC 294: Magomed Ankalaev vs. Johnny Walker ends in controversial no-contest Rozenstruik vs. Gaziev''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 238''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 96''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 87''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 2, 2024, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility, in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/01/ufc-saudi-arabia-date-fight-card-riyadh|title=Saudi Arabia announces new date for inaugural UFC event in Riyadh|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-01-24|access-date=2024-01-24}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event would have marked the promotion's debut in Saudi Arabia.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/10/ufc-saudi-arabia-inaugural-event-march-2024|title=UFC announces inaugural Saudi Arabia event coming in March 2024|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2023-10-11|access-date=2023-10-11}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/10/11/23913292/ufc-books-first-ever-card-in-saudi-arabia-for-march-2024|title=UFC books first card in Saudi Arabia for March 2024|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-10-11|access-date=2023-10-11}} However on January 15, it was reported by [[Ariel Helwani]] that the local organization was seeking a deeper and more high-profile card than was being offered by the UFC and the event was postponed to a later date – possibly in June. At the time of publication, it is unclear if the fights scheduled for the event will still take place at a different location, or if the bouts will be rescheduled for new dates.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/01/ufc-saudi-arabia-postponed-date-riyadh-fight-card|title=Report: UFC Saudi Arabia postponed, possibly until June|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2024-01-15|access-date=2024-01-15}} UFC CEO [[Dana White]] later dismissed the move was due to a \"weak lineup for the event\" and said that \"a couple of fights that we wanted to line up, weren’t ready to go\" as the reason for the postponement, also adding that they \"didn't tell Saudi Arabia about one fight\".{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/01/ufc-ceo-dana-white-denies-saudi-arabia-card-quality-rumors|title=Dana White calls 'bullsh*t' on talk of UFC Saudi Arabia postponement over quality of card|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2024-01-18|access-date=2024-01-18}} \n\nOn January 24, Saudi Arabia's chairman of [[General Entertainment Authority]] [[Turki Alalshikh]] announced the event in [[Riyadh]] (expected to take place at [[Kingdom Arena]]) had been officially postponed until [[UFC on ABC 6|June 22]], citing \"a decision to reschedule was taken to ensure the best caliber of talent will be available to participate\". The March 2 event took place at the UFC Apex in Las Vegas with the same fights originally booked for the event.\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Jairzinho Rozenstruik]] and [[Shamil Gaziev]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/02/ufc-fight-night-jairzinho-rozenstruik-vs-shamil-gaziev-headliner-march-2|title=Jairzinho Rozenstruik vs. Shamil Gaziev to headline March UFC Fight Night event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells and Nolan King|date=2024-02-08|access-date=2024-02-09}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Matt Schnell]] and [[Steve Erceg]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Steve-Erceg-Matt-Schnell-Rebooked-for-UFC-Fight-Night-238-on-March-2-192518|title=Steve Erceg, Matt Schnell rebooked for UFC Fight Night 238 on March 2|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2024-02-04|access-date=2024-02-05}} They were originally scheduled for [[UFC 295]] in November 2023, but Schnell withdrew for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|author=News Desk|date=2023-10-28|title=UFC 295: Matt Schnell out, Alessandro Costa faces Steve Erceg|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/ufc-295-matt-schnell-out-alessandro-costa-faces-steve-erceg/|access-date=2023-10-29|publisher=itnwwe.com}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Josefine Lindgren Knutsson]] and Julia Polastri was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|author=Ashah Tafari |date=2024-01-21|title=Josefine 'Thunder' Knutsson set for next UFC fight|url=https://frontkick.online/senaste-nytt/josefine-knutsson-set-for-a-new-fight-in-the-ufc/ |access-date=2024-01-21|publisher=frontkick.online}} However, Knutsson pulled out due to injury and Polastri was rescheduled to face Stephanie Luciano at [[UFC Fight Night: Ribas vs. Namajunas]].{{Cite web|author=Tobias Lindkvist|date=2024-02-19|title=BREAKING: Josefine Knutsson pulls out of Taveras weighed in at 139.75 pounds, three and three quarters pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit. Gordon weighed in at 127.5 pounds, one and a half pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit. took place on March 9, 2024, at the [[Kaseya Center]] in [[Miami, Florida]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/12/15/24003602/ufc-confirms-event-dates-locations-including-ufc-299-ufc-300|title=UFC confirms event dates, locations including UFC 299, UFC headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2023-12-15|access-date=2023-12-17}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's third visit to Miami and first since [[UFC 287]] in April 2023.\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Sean O'Malley (fighter)|Sean O'Malley]] and [[Marlon Vera]] headlined the event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/.amp/news/sean-omalley-marlon-vera-headline-march-ufc-ppv|title=Sean O'Malley vs. Marlon Vera 2 Set To Headline UFC 299 Pay-Per-View In March|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Curtis Calhoun|date=2023-11-06|access-date=2023-11-06}} The pair previously met at [[UFC 252]] in August 2020 which Vera won by first round TKO.{{cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-252-results-highlights-marlon-vera-stuns-injured-sean-omalley-with-vicious-tko-stoppage/|title=UFC 252 results, highlights: Marlon Vera stuns injured Sean O'Malley with vicious TKO stoppage|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brian Campbell|date=2020-08-16|access-date=2023-11-06}} [[Merab Dvalishvili]] served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/3/5/24091937/merab-dvalishvili-set-to-serve-as-backup-to-ufc-299-main-event|title=Merab Dvalishvili set to serve as backup to UFC 299 main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-03-05|access-date=2024-03-05}}\n\nA five round lightweight bout between former interim [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Dustin Poirier]] and [[Benoît Saint Denis]] took place in the co-main event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/1/8/24029566/dustin-poirier-vs-benoit-saint-denis-set-for-five-round-co-main-event-at-ufc-299|title=Dustin Poirier vs. Benoit Saint Denis set for five-round co-main event at UFC 299|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-01-07|access-date=2024-01-07}} The bout was briefly in jeopardy when Poirier indicated the bout would not take place as he had never signed a contract and he was unable to come to terms with the promotion.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/02/ufc-299-dustin-poirier-vs-benoit-saint-denis-canceled|title=Dustin Poirier says UFC 299 fight vs. Benoit Saint-Denis is off: 'We couldn't come to terms'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2024-02-01|access-date=2024-02-01}} However, Poirier shortly thereafter retracted his statement describing it as a misunderstanding.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/2/1/24058561/dustin-poirier-apologizes-misunderstanding-declares-fight-with-benoit-saint-denis-back-on-ufc-299|title=Dustin Poirier apologizes for 'misunderstanding,' declares fight with Benoit Saint Denis back on for UFC 299|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-02-01|access-date=2024-02-01}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Curtis Blaydes]] and [[Jailton Almeida]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/12/12/23998755/jailton-almeida-vs-curtis-blaydes-rebooked-for-ufc-299-in-march|title=Jailton Almeida vs. Curtis Blaydes rebooked for UFC 299 in March|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-12-12|access-date=2023-12-12}} They were previously expected to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Almeida vs. Lewis]] in November 2023 but Blaydes withdrew for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/10/ufc-news-derrick-lewis-vs-jailton-almeida-sao-paulo-curtis-blaydes-out-mma-fight-booking-matchup|title=Derrick Lewis replaces Curtis Blaydes, headlines UFC Sao Paulo vs. Jailton Almeida|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2023-10-09|access-date=2023-12-12}}\n\n[[Ian Machado Garry]] and [[Geoff Neal]] were expected to meet in a welterweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/12/ufc-299-news-ian-machado-garry-vs-geoff-neal-miami-pay-per-view-welterweight-grudge-match|title=After UFC 296 withdrawal, Ian Machado Garry gets booking against Geoff Neal at UFC 299|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2023-12-17|access-date=2023-12-17}} They were originally booked for [[UFC 292]] in August 2023, but Neal withdrew due to undisclosed health reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/08/ufc-292-geoff-neal-out-of-ufc-292-fight-vs-ian-machado-garry-boston-health-issues|title=Geoff Neal out of UFC 292 fight vs. Ian Machado Garry in Boston|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2023-08-09|access-date=2023-08-09}} However the bout was moved to [[UFC 298]] for unknown reasons.{{Cite event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 30, 2024, at [[Boardwalk Hall]] in [[Atlantic City, New Jersey]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/12/15/24003602/ufc-confirms-event-dates-locations-including-ufc-299-ufc-300|title=UFC confirms event dates, locations including UFC 299, UFC Fight promoter is returning to Atlantic City next year. What to know|publisher=courierpostonline.com|author=J. Staas Haught|date=2023-12-16|access-date=2023-12-16}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's tenth visit to Atlantic City and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Barboza vs. Lee]] in April 2018.\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Erin Blanchfield]] and [[Manon Fiorot]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/2/15/24073811/erin-blanchfield-vs-manon-fiorot-promoted-to-ufc-atlantic-city-main-event|title=Erin Blanchfield vs. Manon Fiorot promoted to UFC Atlantic City main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2024-02-15|access-date=2024-02-15}} The bout was promoted to main event status when the original announced headliner, a welterweight bout between [[Sean Brady (fighter)|Sean Brady]] and [[Vicente Luque]], fell through when Brady indicated he never signed a bout agreement due to dealing with an injury.{{Cite web|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2024-01-12|title=Vicente Luque vs. Sean Brady headlines UFC Atlantic City event|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/1/12/24036690/vicente-luque-vs-sean-brady-headlines-ufc-atlantic-city-event|access-date=2024-01-12|publisher=mmafighting.com}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/01/ufc-atlantic-city-loses-sean-brady-vs-vicente-luque-main-event|title=UFC Atlantic City loses headliner as Sean Brady vs. Vicente Luque fizzles|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King, Mike Bohn, and Farah Hannoun|date=2024-01-31|access-date=2024-01-31}} Luque was instead matched up with [[Joaquin Buckley]] in the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/02/ufc-news-vicente-luque-remains-atlantic-city-co-main-event-vs-joaquin-buckley|title=Vicente Luque remains on UFC Atlantic City with co-main event vs. Joaquin Buckley|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2024-02-15|access-date=2024-02-15}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Dominick Reyes]] and [[Carlos Ulberg]] was scheduled [[UFC 297]], but it was postponed due to an injury sustained by Ulberg and was rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/01/ufc-fight-night-dominick-reyes-vs-carlos-ulberg-rebooked-atlantic-city|title=Dominick Reyes vs. Carlos Ulberg rebooked for UFC Atlantic City in March|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2024-01-09|access-date=2024-01-09}} However, Reyes pulled out due to [[deep vein thrombosis]] and was replaced by [[Alonzo Menifield]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/1/23/24048536/dominick-reyes-out-alonzo-menifield-now-faces-carlos-ulberg-at-ufc-atlantic-city|title=Dominick Reyes out, Alonzo Menifield now faces Carlos Nascimento|UFC on ESPN 56]] on May 11 due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/02/ufc-news-alonzo-menifield-vs-carlos-ulberg|title=Alonzo Menifield vs. Carlos '''''UFC on ESPN+ 95''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on February 24, 2024, at [[Arena CDMX]], in [[Mexico City]], Mexico.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/12/15/24003602/ufc-confirms-event-dates-locations-including-ufc-299-ufc-300|title=UFC confirms event dates, locations including UFC 299, UFC 300|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2023-12-15|access-date=2023-12-16}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's sixth visit to Mexico City and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Rodríguez vs. Stephens]] in September 2019.\n\nA flyweight bout between former two-time [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight Champion]] [[Brandon Moreno]] and [[Amir Albazi]] was originally expected to serve as a five-round co-main event, but was elevated as the event's headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/12/ufc-news-brandon-moreno-meets-amir-albazi-five-rounds-flyweight-mexico-city-return|title=Brandon Moreno meets Amir Albazi in five-round co-main event in UFC's expected Mexico City return|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2023-12-02|access-date=2023-12-02}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/dana-white/dana-white-announces-several-new-fights-including-three-for-ufc-300/|title=Dana White announces several new fights including three for UFC 300|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Cole Shelton|date=2023-12-21|access-date=2023-12-21}} However, Albazi withdrew due to a neck injury and was replaced by former title challenger (also former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Flyweight Championship|LFA Flyweight Champion]]) [[Brandon Royval]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/1/11/24035284/ufc-mexico-city-gets-new-headliner-after-amir-albazi-forced-off-card|title=UFC Mexico City gets new headliner after Amir Albazi forced off card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steve Marrocco|date=2024-01-11|access-date=2024-01-11}} The pair previously met at [[UFC 255]] in November 2020 which Moreno won by first round TKO.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2020/11/21/21588572/watch-ufc-255-full-fight-video-highlights-moreno-knockout-royval-results-espn-mma-ppv-shoulder|title=Highlights! Brandon Moreno earns stoppage after Brandon Royval separates shoulder at UFC 255|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2020-11-21|access-date=2024-01-11}} Moreno and Albazi were later scheduled to meet in November 2024 at [[UFC Fight Night: Moreno vs. Albazi]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/9/3/24235265/brandon-moreno-vs-amir-albazi-set-as-new-main-event-for-ufc-edmonton|title=Brandon Moreno vs. Amir Albazi set as new main event for UFC Edmonton|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-09-03|access-date=2024-09-03}}\n\nA featherweight rematch between former interim [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner) [[Yair Rodríguez]] and former title challenger [[Brian Ortega]] took place in the co-main event, which marked the sixth time a non-main event and non-title bout has been scheduled for five rounds.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/12/ufc-news-yair-rodriguez-vs-brian-ortega-2-headline-fight-night-event-mexico|title=Yair Rodriguez vs. Brian Ortega 2 co-headlines UFC Fight Night in Mexico City with five rounds|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2023-12-21|access-date=2023-12-21}} The pairing previously met at [[UFC on ABC: Ortega vs. Rodríguez]] which Rodríguez won by first round TKO due to a shoulder injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/7/16/23237908/yair-rodriguez-wins-after-brian-ortega-suffers-brutal-dislocated-shoulder-ufc-long-island-main-event|title=Yair Rodriguez wins after Brian Ortega suffers brutal dislocated shoulder in UFC Long Island main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-07-16|access-date=2023-12-21}} \n\nA flyweight rematch between [[Édgar Cháirez]] and Daniel Lacerda took place the event.{{Cite web|author=Vipin Raghuwanshi|date=2023-11-20|title=Edgar Chairez vs Daniel Lacerda rebooked for UFC February 24|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/edgar-chairez-vs-daniel-lacerda-rebooked-for-ufc-february-24/ |access-date=2023-11-20|publisher=itnwwe.com}} The pair had previously fought at [[UFC Fight Night: Grasso vs. Shevchenko 2]] in September 2023, where the bout was ruled a no-contest due to premature referee stoppage.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/09/noche-ufc-video-bad-referee-stoppage-leads-to-no-contest-in-edgar-chairez-vs-daniel-lacerda|title=Noche UFC video: Bad referee stoppage leads to no Barboza]] two months later, but the bout was cancelled due to Lacerda suffering medical issues after the weigh-in.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-10-13|title=UFC Vegas 81 loses Edgar Chairez vs. Daniel Lacerda rematch due to medical issue|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/10/13/ufc-vegas-81-loses-edgar-chairez-daniel-lacerda/|access-date=2023-10-14|publisher=cagesidepress.com}} At the weigh ins, both fighters missed weight. Cháirez weighed in at 131 pounds and Lacerda at 127 pounds, five pounds and one pound over the flyweight non-title fight limit, respectively. Their bout proceeded at catchweight with both fighters fined a percentage of their purse.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/02/ufc-fight-night-237-mexico-city-brandon-moreno-vs-brandon-royval-ortega-rodriguez-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC Fight Night 237 weigh-in results: Two fighters miss mark, including five-pound botch|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2024-02-23|access-date=2024-02-23}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Raul Rosas Jr.]] and [[The Return of The Ultimate Fighter: Team Volkanovski vs. Team Ortega|The Ultimate Fighter 29]] bantamweight winner Ricky Turcios was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/1/5/24027412/raul-rosas-jr-faces-ricky-turcios-at-ufc-mexico-city|title=Raul Rosas Jr. faces Ricky Turcios at UFC Mexico City|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2024-01-05|access-date=2024-02-24}} However, Rosas Jr. was forced withdraw just before the bout was expected to begin due to an illness. locations including UFC 299, UFC 300|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2023-12-15|access-date=2023-12-16}}\n\n==Background==\n===Main card: three title fights and a title eliminator===\n[[File:T-Mobile Arena (31534778122).jpg|thumb|left|250px|After hosting [[UFC 200]] in 2016, [[T-Mobile Arena (Las Vegas)|T-Mobile Arena]] hosted another milestone event, which was its 28th UFC event overall.{{Cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/2794903|title=UFC 300 to take place April 13 in Las Vegas|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2023-12-15|access-date=2023-12-22}}]]\n\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Alex Pereira]] (also former [[List of UFC champions#Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] and former [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Middleweight Championship|Glory Middleweight]] and [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]]) and former champion [[Jamahal Hill]] headlined the event.{{Cite title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-02-18|access-date=2024-02-18}} The fight was verbally agreed for [[UFC 301]] three weeks later, but the promotion opted to book it at this card.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/02/ufc-300-alex-pereira-signs-news-contract-says-fight-bumped-up-brazil|title=Alex Pereira signs new 8-fight contract before UFC 300, says Procházka]] after Hill was forced to vacate it in July 2023 due to a ruptured [[Achilles tendon]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/7/14/23791259/jamahal-hill-relinquishes-ufc-light-heavyweight-title-suffering-ruptured-achilles-tendon-basketball|title=Jamahal Hill relinquishes UFC title heavyweight title at UFC 295|publisher=bleacherreport.com|author=Alex Ballentine|date=2023-11-12|access-date=2024-02-18}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout between current two-time champion [[Zhang Weili]] and [[Yan Xiaonan]] took place in the co-main event. It marked the first time two Chinese-born athletes met for a title in the organization.{{Cite Gaethje]] (also former [[WSOF Lightweight Championship|WSOF Lightweight Champion]]) put his symbolic \"BMF\" (''baddest [[motherfucker]]'') title on the line against former [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Max Holloway]] in a lightweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/platform/amp/2024/1/16/24040941/ufc-300-justin-gaethje-defends-bmf-belt-vs-max-holloway-jim-miller-gets-bobby-green|title=UFC 300: Justin Gaethje defends BMF belt Miller became the only fighter to have competed at all three milestone events for the organization, previously competing at [[UFC 100]] and [[UFC 200]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/01/ufc-300-fight-card-jim-miller-vs-bobby-green|title=Jim Miller gets UFC 300 wish, fights fellow vet Bobby Green at historic [[UFC 172]] in April 2014, due to an elbow injury;{{cite web|url=http://www.mmatorch.com/artman2/publish/UFC_2/article_21134.shtml|title=With Bobby Green injured, UFC 172 shakes up lightweight bouts with Miller-Medeiros and Ellenberger-Rocha now booked|publisher=mmatorch.com|author=Jamie Penick|date=2014-04-20|access-date=2021-02-01}} the second booking at [[UFC 258]] in February 2021, when he collapsed after the weigh-ins and was deemed unfit to compete;{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/ufc-258-bobby-green-collapses-after-weigh-ins|title=Bobby Green collapses after UFC 258 weigh-ins, pulled from Jim Miller fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2021-02-12|access-date=2021-02-12}} and the third time at [[UFC 276]] in July 2022, when Green withdrew for unknown reasons.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/06/ufc-276-donald-cerrone-vs-jim-miller-bobby-green-injury-mma-matchup-booking|title=Donald Cerrone replaces Bobby event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/deiveson-figueiredo-booked-bantamweight-return-ufc-300|title=Deiveson Figueiredo booked for bantamweight return at UFC 300 against a former Championship]] challenger) was the next \"fight wing\" [[UFC Hall of Fame]] inductee. Anderson defended the title via fifth-round submission in what was considered one of the greatest comebacks in MMA history.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/04/anderson-silva-vs-chael-sonnen-1-ufc-117-joins-2024-hall-of-fame-class-fight-wing|title=Anderson Silva vs. Chael Sonnen 1 joins 2024 UFC Hall of Fame class|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2024-04-14|access-date=2024-04-14}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout \n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Alex Pereira]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Jamahal Hill]]\n|KO (punches)\n|1\n|3:14\n|For the [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout \n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Zhang Weili]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Yan Xiaonan]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (49–45, 49–45, 49–45)\n|5\n|5:00\n|For the [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout \n|Lightweight\n|[[Max Holloway]]\n|def.\n|[[Justin Gaethje]] (c)\n|KO (punch)\n|5\n|4:59\n|For the symbolic \"BMF\" title. Gaethje'''\n*'''Performance of the Night: Max Holloway''' and '''Jiří Procházka'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nThe Nevada State Athletic Commission (NSAC) has withheld the purses of [[Arman Tsarukyan]] and [[Diego Lopes (fighter)|Diego Lopes]] due to their behavior at the event, where Arman Tsarukyan allegedly struck a fan on his way to the octagon, and Diego Lopes for jumping out of the cage.{{Cite web|url=https://mmauncensored.com/2024/04/26/nsac-ufc-300-purses-tsarukyan-lopes/|title=NSAC to hold hearings on withholding UFC 300 purses for Arman Tsarukyan and Diego Lopes|author=|date=2024-04-26|publisher=mmauncensored}} Both fighters met with the NSAC on April 30, 2024, to discuss the incidents.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2024/4/26/24141307/nac-to-discuss-withholding-of-purse-for-ufc-300-fighters-arman-tsarukyan-and-diego-lopes|title=NSAC to discuss 'withholding of purse' for UFC 300 fighters Arman Tsarukyan and Diego Lopes|author=|date=2024-04-16|publisher=mmamania}} The NSAC decided to withhold 20% of Tsarukyan's fight purse ($31,600 of $158,000) and $5,000 of Lopes' $100,000 payout.{{Cite web |date=2024-04-30 |title=Arman Tsarukyan & Diego Lopes Face UFC 300 Repercussions; Fighter Fined for Infamous Bite |url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/ufc/ufc-300-tsarukyan-lopes-fined |access-date=2024-05-13 |website=MMA Knockout |language=en-US}}\n\nThis event won the [[2024 in UFC#2024 UFC Honors awards|UFC Honors ''2024 Event of the Year'']] award as voted by the fans.{{Cite web|url=https://www.homeoffight.com/news/ufc/ufc-honors-2024-event-recap|title=UFC Honors 2024 Event Recap|publisher=homeoffight.com|author=Mike Jones|date=2025-01-13|access-date=2025-01-15|archive-date=2025-02-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20250207104833/https://www.homeoffight.com/news/ufc/ufc-honors-2024-event-recap|url-status=dead}}\n\n== and [[Duško Todorović]] were expected to meet in a middleweight bout at the event. Dobson.{{Cite web|url=https://sportklub.n1info.rs/sk-fight/dusko-todorovic-ufc-mma-otkazana-borba-povreda-koleno/|title=A big pity: Another serious injury to Todorovic and the match was cancelled|publisher=sportklub.n1info.rs|author=Nikola Novaković|date=2024-02-14|access-date=2024-02-17}} {{in lang|sr}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Edmen-Shahbazyan-vs-AJ-Dobson-Slated-for-UFC-on-ESPN-53-on-March-23-192617|title=Edmen Shahbazyan vs. A.J. when Luciano was forced to withdraw due to falling ill with [[dengue fever]].{{Cite following fighters received $50,000 bonuses.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc-vegas-89/ufc-vegas-89-bonus-report-five-fighters-take-home-an-extra-50k/|title=UFC Vegas 89 Bonus Report: the Night: Payton Talbott''' and '''Fernando Padilla'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nAfter the first ever disqualification due to biting took place at this event, UFC CEO [[Dana White]] gave a ''Bite of the Night'' $50,000 bonus to [[André Lima (fighter)|André Lima]] whose bout ended prematurely as a result of being bit on the arm by opponent [[Igor Severino]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-espn-53-bonuses-andre-lima-bite-50000-tattoo|title=Andre Lima's oh-so-Vegas tattoo decision after UFC on ESPN 53 made him an extra $50k|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=2024-03-23|access-date=2024-03-23}} Severino was subsequently released from his contract with the promotion.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/3/23/24110056/report-igor-severino-to-be-cut-from-ufc-after-biting-andre-lima-at-ufc-vegas-89|title=Igor Severino to be cut from UFC after biting Andre Lima at UFC Vegas 89|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2024-03-23|access-date=2024-03-23}} The [[Nevada Athletic Commission]] also announced that it would withhold Severino's purse and he is expected to receive a heavy fine and lengthy suspension when the NAC holds its monthly meeting in April.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/03/ufc-igor-severino-bite-purse-withheld-nevada-commission|title=Igor Severino's UFC on ESPN 53 bite leads Nevada commission to withhold purse|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano and Nolan King|date=2024-03-25|access-date=2024-03-25}}\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Arts}}\n*[[2024 in UFC]]\n*[[List of current UFC As a result, her bout with former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Women's Strawweight Championship|LFA Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Piera Rodríguez]] was cancelled.\n*Nora Cornolle and Melissa Mullins weighed in at 138.5 and 138 pounds, 2 and a half and 2 pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit respectively. arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on April 27, 2024, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility, in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/03/ufc-news-manel-kape-vs-matheus-nicolau-2-april-17-fight-night-headliner|title=Manel Kape vs. Matheus Nicolau 2 set to headline UFC Fight Night in April|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells and Nolan King|date=2024-03-08|access-date=2024-03-08}}\n\n==Background==\nA flyweight rematch between former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Bantamweight Championship|Rizin Bantamweight Champion]] [[Manel Kape]] and [[Matheus Nicolau]] was expected to headline the event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2024/3/7/24093937/matheus-nicolau-vs-manel-kape-2-rebooked-to-headline-ufc-vegas-91-mma|title=Matheus Nicolau vs. Manel Kape 2 rebooked to headline UFC Vegas 91|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Drake Riggs and Alex Behunin|date=2024-03-07|access-date=2024-03-07}} The pairing previously met at [[UFC Fight Night: Edwards vs. Muhammad]] in March 2021 which Nicolau won by controversial split decision.{{cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/03/13/ufc-vegas-21-results-matheus-nicolau-vs-manel-kape/|title=UFC Vegas 21 Results: Matheus Nicolau Wins Razor-Thin Decision Against Manel Kape|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2021-03-13|access-date=2024-03-07}} Their second meeting was previously scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Ankalaev vs. Walker 2]] in January 2024 but Kape missed weight and the bout was subsequently cancelled.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/1/12/24036159/ufc-vegas-84-manel-kape-vs-matheus-nicolau-cancelled-after-kape-weight-miss|title=UFC Vegas 84: Manel Kape vs. Matheus Nicolau off; Kape claims Nicolau refused catchweight fight|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2024-01-12|access-date=2024-03-07}} In turn, Kape pulled out due to a rib injury and was replaced by former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Alex Perez (fighter)|Alex Perez]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/4/2/24119555/manel-kape-out-matheus-nicolau-vs-alex-perez-announced-as-new-ufc-vegas-91-headliner|title=Manel Kape out, Matheus Nicolau vs. Alex Perez Maheshate Hayisaer was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|author=News Desk|date=2024-01-20|title=Anshul Jubli next fight reported vs Maheshate Hayisaer for UFC April by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 22, 2024, at the [[Kingdom Arena]] in [[Riyadh]], Saudi Arabia.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/01/ufc-saudi-arabia-date-fight-card-riyadh|title=Saudi Arabia announces new date for inaugural UFC event in Riyadh|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-01-24|access-date=2024-01-24}}\n\n==Background==\nThe promotion was originally expected to make its Saudi Arabia debut at an [[UFC Fight Night: Rozenstruik vs. Gaziev|event on March 2, 2024]],{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/10/ufc-saudi-arabia-inaugural-event-march-2024|title=UFC announces inaugural Saudi Arabia event coming in March 2024|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2023-10-11|access-date=2023-10-11}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/10/11/23913292/ufc-books-first-ever-card-in-saudi-arabia-for-march-2024|title=UFC books first card in Saudi Arabia for March 2024|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-10-11|access-date=2023-10-11}} but on January 24, Saudi Arabia's chairman of [[General Entertainment Authority]] [[Turki Alalshikh]] announced the event had been officially postponed until this date, citing \"a decision to reschedule was taken to ensure the best caliber of talent will be available to participate\".\n\nA middleweight title eliminator bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: The Smashes]] welterweight winner) [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] and [[Khamzat Chimaev]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/3/28/24114291/khamzat-chimaev-vs-robert-whittaker-set-main-event-4-more-fights-announced-ufc-saudi-arabia-june-22|title=Khamzat Chimaev vs. Robert Whittaker set as main event, 4 more fights announced for UFC Saudi Arabia on June 22|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-03-28|access-date=2024-03-28}} However, Chimaev withdrew due to an illness and was replaced by [[Ikram Aliskerov]], who was scheduled to fight a week earlier at [[UFC on ESPN: Perez vs. Taira]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/06/ufc-saudi-arabia-khamzat-chimaev-out-robert-whittaker-vs-ikram-aliskerov-set-new-abc-6-headliner|title=Khamzat Chimaev out; Robert Whittaker vs. Ikram Aliskerov set as new UFC on ABC 6 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2024-06-13|access-date=2024-06-13}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Sharabutdin Magomedov]] and Ihor Potieria was expected to take place at the event. However, Potieria was pulled to face [[Michel Pereira]] at [[UFC 301]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/4/9/24125057/ihor-potieria-steps-in-fight-michel-pereira-ufc-301|title=Ihor Potieria steps in to fight Michel Pereira at UFC 301|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2024-04-09|access-date=2024-04-09}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer Joilton Lutterbach.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/05/ufc-saudi-arabia-shara-bullet-magomedov-vs-joilton-lutterbach-mma-matchup-booking-announcement|title='Bullet' booking: Shara Magomedov gets 48-fight veteran newcomer for UFC Saudi Arabia|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2024-05-20|access-date=2024-05-20}} In turn, Lutterbach was pulled out on June 19 by the anti-doping commission because of a substance found in his urine and replaced by Antonio Trócoli, who was scheduled to face Aliskerov a week earlier.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/06/shara-magomedov-new-opponent-antonio-trocoli-ufc-on-abc-6-riyadh-saudi-arabia|title=Shara Magomedov gets new opponent for Saturday's UFC on ABC 6|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2024-06-19|access-date=2024-06-19}}\n\n[[Said Nurmagomedov]] was scheduled to face [[Montel Jackson]] in a bantamweight bout.{{cite Ige at UFC Fight Night on July 20|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/06/chepe-mariscal-steps-in-vs-dan-ige-at-ufc-fight-night-on-july-20|access-date=2024-06-26|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn}} Subsequently, reasons and replaced by [[Road to UFC Season 2]] flyweight winner Rei Tsuruya.{{Cite web|publisher=sherdog.com|title=Flyweight prospect Rei Tsuruya slated to face Carlos Hernandez at UFC (fighter)|Diego Lopes]] was changed to a lightweight bout at the request of Ortega, reportedly due to weight-cutting issues. Center]] in [[Louisville, Kentucky]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/04/ufc-louisville-fight-card-date-tickets|title=UFC Louisville official for June at KFC Yum! Center|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-04-17|access-date=2024-04-17}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's second visit to Louisville and first since [[UFC Live: Sanchez vs. Kampmann]] in March 2011.\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Jared Cannonier]] and [[Nassourdine Imavov]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2024/4/25/24140458/jared-cannonier-vs-nassourdine-imamov-headlines-ufc-louisville-june-8-mma|title=Jared Cannonier vs Nassourdine Imamov headlines UFC Louisville on June 8|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland and Alex Behunin|date=2024-04-25|access-date=2024-04-25}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Raul Rosas Jr.]] and [[The Return of The Ultimate Fighter: Team Volkanovski vs. Team Ortega]] bantamweight winner Ricky Turcios took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/04/ufc-news-raul-rosas-jr-vs-ricky-turcios-rebooking|title=UFC rebooks Raul Rosas Jr. vs. Ricky Turcios|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King, Danny Segura and Farah Hannoun|date=2024-04-11|access-date=2024-04-11}} They were originally scheduled to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Moreno vs. Royval 2]] on February 24, but Rosas Jr. was forced to withdraw just before the bout was expected to begin due to an illness. As a result, the pairing was initially pushed back one week to [[UFC Fight Night: Rozenstruik vs. Gaziev]] before it was ultimately cancelled by the promotion.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/2/24/24082648/raul-rosas-jr-vs-ricky-turcios-scrapped-from-ufc-mexico-city-just-before-walkouts|title=Raul Rosas Jr. vs. Ricky Turcios scrapped from UFC Mexico City just before walkouts|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-02-24|access-date=2024-02-24}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/2/27/24085182/raul-rosas-jr-vs-ricky-turcios-no-longer-expected-at-ufc-vegas-87-on-saturday|title=Raul Rosas Jr. vs. Ricky Turcios no longer expected at UFC Vegas 87 on Saturday|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-02-27|access-date=2024-02-27}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Roman Dolidze]] and [[Michel Pereira]] was briefly announced as part of the event.{{Cite web|author=Drake Riggs|date=2024-05-09|title=Roman Dolidze vs. Michel Pereira added to UFC Louisville|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2024/5/9/24153146/roman-dolidze-vs-michel-pereira-added-to-ufc-louisville-mma|access-date=2024-05-15|publisher=mmamania.com}} However, Pereira claimed he never signed any contracts for the fight and the bout fell through.{{Cite web|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2024-05-10|title=Michel Pereira explains why he won't fight Roman Dolidze at UFC Louisville|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/5/10/24153812/michel-pereira-explains-wont-fight-roman-dolidze-ufc-louisville|access-date=2024-05-15|publisher=mmafighting.com}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Eduarda Moura weighed in at 116.5 pounds, half a pound over the women's strawweight non-title fight limit. martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 15, 2024, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility, in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/05/ufc-news-tatsuro-taira-vs-alex-perez-main-event-booked-june-15|title=Rising Japanese star Tatsuro Taira books first UFC main event vs. Alex Perez on June 15|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2024-05-13|access-date=2024-05-13}}\n\n==Background==\nThis was the 100th event the promotion hosted at the [[UFC Apex]]. The UFC started hosting events at the venue in [[UFC on ESPN: Woodley vs. Burns|May 2020]] due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]], originally [[Behind closed doors (sport)|behind closed doors]], before changing their approach to a limited set of tickets per event in [[UFC on ESPN: Sandhagen vs. Dillashaw|July 2021]].\n\nA flyweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Alex Perez (fighter)|Alex Perez]] and [[Tatsuro Taira]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Drake Riggs|date=2024-05-13|title=Shake-up! UFC Vegas 93 re-arranges matchups, Alex Perez vs. Tatsuro Taira now headlines|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2024/5/13/24155839/shake-up-ufc-vegas-93-re-arranges-matchups-alex-perez-vs-tatsuro-taira-now-headlines-mma|access-date=2024-05-13|publisher=mmamania.com}} Perez had originally been scheduled to face [[Tagir Ulanbekov]],{{Cite web|author=Vipin Raghuwanshi|date=2024-03-22|title=Alex Perez vs Tagir Ulanbekov reported for UFC event on June 15|url=https://www.itnwwe.com/mma/alex-perez-vs-tagir-ulanbekov-reported-for-ufc-event-on-june-15/|access-date=2024-03-22|publisher=itnwwe.com}} but he was pulled from that matchup to serve as a replacement and headline [[UFC on ESPN: Nicolau vs. Perez]] in April.{{cite web|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2024-04-02|title=Manel Kape out, Matheus Nicolau vs. Alex Perez announced as new UFC Vegas 91 headliner|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/4/2/24119555/manel-kape-out-matheus-nicolau-vs-alex-perez-announced-as-new-ufc-vegas-91-headliner|access-date=2024-04-02|publisher=mmafighting.com}} On April 30, it was reported that the bout between Perez and Ulanbekov had been rebooked for this event.{{cite news|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2024-04-30|title=Alex Perez vs. Tagir Ulanbekov back on for UFC Fight Night on June 15|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/04/ufc-news-alex-perez-vs-tagir-ulanbekov-rebooked-june-15|access-date=2024-04-30|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}} Taira, meanwhile, had been slated to step in as a replacement at [[UFC 302]] two weeks earlier against [[Joshua Van]] before being moved to the main event here.{{Cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/04/ufc-302-tatsuro-taira-vs-joshua-van-fight-card|title=Tatsuro Taira fills UFC 302 opening, fights Joshua Van|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-04-25|access-date=2024-04-25}}\n\nAfter Perez was shifted into the headliner role, the promotion instead paired Ulanbekov with Van, who had briefly been linked as Taira's opponent at UFC 302. However, the bout was cancelled when Ulanbekov weighed in at 129.5 pounds, three and a half pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit.{{Cite web|date=2024-06-14|title=UFC on ESPN 58 weigh-in results: Ranked flyweight misses mark by 3.5 pounds, fight women's strawweight bout between former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Women's Strawweight Championship|LFA Women's Strawweight Champion]] Julia Polastri and [[Josefine Lindgren Knutsson]] took place [[UFC on ESPN: Lemos vs. Jandiroba]] \n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Namajunas vs. Cortez''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 59''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 13, 2024, at the [[Ball Arena]] in [[Denver, Colorado]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/05/ufc-denver-fight-card-news-ball-arena|title=UFC lays groundwork for Denver return in July|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-05-31|access-date=2024-05-31}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/ufc/story/_/id/40281185/rose-namajunas-maycee-barber-headlines-fight-night-denver|title=Rose Namajunas-Maycee Barber headlines Fight Night in Denver|publisher=espn.com|author=Andreas Hale|date=2024-06-04|access-date=2024-06-05}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's seventh visit to Denver and first since [[UFC Fight Night: The Korean Zombie vs. Rodríguez]] in November 2018.\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former two-time [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Rose Namajunas]] and [[Maycee Barber]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|publisher=sherdog.com|title=Rose Namajunas, Maycee Barber slated to headline UFC card on July 13|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Rose-Namajunas-Maycee-Barber-Slated-to-Headline-UFC-Card-on-July-13-193681|date=2024-05-14|access-date=2024-05-14|author=Tristen Critchfield}} However, Barber withdrew from the bout, due to what was later revealed to be ongoing health problems, and was replaced by [[Tracy Cortez]], who was scheduled to face [[Miranda Maverick]] one week later at [[UFC on ESPN: Lemos vs. Jandiroba]].{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-06-25|title=Maycee Barber out; Tracy Cortez expected to face Rose Namajunas at UFC Denver|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/6/25/24186089/maycee-barber-out-tracy-cortez-expected-to-face-rose-namajunas-at-ufc-denver|access-date=2024-06-26|publisher=mmafighting.com}}{{Cite web|author=Chris Taylor|date=2024-06-29 |title=Maycee Barber explains why she pulled out of UFC Denver main event with Rose Namajunas|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/maycee-barber/maycee-barber-explains-why-she-pulled-out-of-ufc-denver-main-event-with-rose-namajunas/|access-date=2024-06-29|publisher=bjpenn.com}}\n\n[[Chris Duncan (fighter)|Chris Duncan]] was scheduled to face [[Nazim Sadykhov]] in a lightweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/?p=189939|title=Chris Duncan to meet Nazim Sadykhov at UFC Denver|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Bryson Hester|date=2024-06-09|access-date=2024-06-10}} However, Duncan was replaced by Marquel Mederos due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/6/18/24180507/morning-report-chael-sonnen-doesnt-believe-conor-mcgregor-injury-hes-known-about-this-for-over-year|title=Morning Report: Chael Sonnen doesn’t believe Conor McGregor injury: ‘He’s known about this for over a year’|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Drake Riggs|date=2024-06-18|access-date=2024-06-19}} A week later, Mederos pulled out and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/06/ufc-denver-fight-card-main-event-lineup-changes|title=UFC Denver fight card loses two more bouts|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-06-26|access-date=2024-06-26}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Drew Dober]] and [[Mike Davis (fighter)|Mike Davis]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/06/ufc-books-drew-dober-vs-mike-davis-july-13-denver|title=UFC adds Drew Dober vs. Mike Davis to July 13 lineup in Denver|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2024-06-05|access-date=2024-06-19}} However, Davis was forced to withdraw due to a torn bicep.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-denver-takes-major-hit-after-fighter-suffers-gruesome-training-injury-|title=UFC Denver takes major hit after fighter suffers gruesome training injury|publisher=si.com|author=Drew Beaupre|date=2024-06-18|access-date=2024-06-19}} He was replaced by [[Jean Silva]], who just fought two weeks earlier at [[UFC 303]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/07/ufc-on-espn-59-drew-dober-vs-jean-lightweight-matchup-denver-ball-arena|title=Drew Dober vs. Jean Silva added to UFC on ESPN 59 in Denver|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2024-07-03|access-date=2024-07-03}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Mike Malott]] and Gilbert Urbina was scheduled to take place at this event.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|date=2024-04-25|title=Mike Malott draws Gilbert Urbina for UFC's July 13 event|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/04/25/mike-malott-draws-gilbert-urbina-for-ufcs-july-13-event/|access-date=2024-06-26|publisher=cagesidepress.com}} However, two weeks before the event, as a result of Urbina withdrawing due to undisclosed reasons, Malott decided not to fight another opponent at this event due to an injury sustained during training, so the bout was scrapped.\n\n[[Viviane Araújo (fighter)|Viviane Araújo]] and [[Jasmine Jasudavicius]] were expected to meet in a women's flyweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/04/ufc-news-viviane-araujo-vs-jasmine-jasdavicious-july-13-fight-night-women-mma-matchup-booking|title=Viviane Araujo vs. Jasmine Jasdavicious teed up for UFC's July 13 Fight Night event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2024-04-08|access-date=2024-07-05}} However on July 5, Araújo pulled out for unknown reasons and was replaced by promotional newcomer Fatima Kline.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/07/ufc-news-fatima-kline-vs-jasmine-jasudavicius-denver|title=UFC signs Fatima Kline for short-notice debut vs.
